══╡ EXCEPTION CAUGHT BY WIDGETS LIBRARY ╞═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
The following assertion was thrown while finalizing the widget tree:
_MobileScannerViewState#c841f(ticker active) was disposed with an active Ticker.
_MobileScannerViewState created a Ticker via its SingleTickerProviderStateMixin, but at the time
dispose() was called on the mixin, that Ticker was still active. The Ticker must be disposed before
calling super.dispose().
Tickers used by AnimationControllers should be disposed by calling dispose() on the
AnimationController itself. Otherwise, the ticker will leak.
The offending ticker was:
  Ticker(created by _MobileScannerViewState#c841f)

══╡ EXCEPTION CAUGHT BY WIDGETS LIBRARY ╞═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
The following assertion was thrown while finalizing the widget tree:
_MobileScannerViewState#3cbef(ticker active) was disposed with an active Ticker.
_MobileScannerViewState created a Ticker via its SingleTickerProviderStateMixin, but at the time
dispose() was called on the mixin, that Ticker was still active. The Ticker must be disposed before
calling super.dispose().
Tickers used by AnimationControllers should be disposed by calling dispose() on the
AnimationController itself. Otherwise, the ticker will leak.
The offending ticker was:

_MobileScannerViewState#558c1(ticker active) was disposed with an active Ticker.
_MobileScannerViewState created a Ticker via its SingleTickerProviderStateMixin, but at the time
dispose() was called on the mixin, that Ticker was still active. The Ticker must be disposed before
calling super.dispose().
Tickers used by AnimationControllers should be disposed by calling dispose() on the
AnimationController itself. Otherwise, the ticker will leak.

The following assertion was thrown while finalizing the widget tree:
_MobileScannerViewState#dac6a(ticker active) was disposed with an active Ticker.
_MobileScannerViewState created a Ticker via its SingleTickerProviderStateMixin, but at the time
dispose() was called on the mixin, that Ticker was still active. The Ticker must be disposed before
calling super.dispose().
Tickers used by AnimationControllers should be disposed by calling dispose() on the
AnimationController itself. Otherwise, the ticker will leak.
